Un-nesting it makes TLS orthogonal to optimization level, which is what the requirements call for.\n\n**Verification notes.** The stale `.make-settings` from the prior `-O3` build pins `OPT=-O3`, so I ran `make distclean` before each of the four builds; without that, a plain `make` would have inherited `-O3` and reported a false pass.\n\n`DEFAULT_HAS_FLTO` and `O3_HAS_FLTO` are measured from LTO IR actually present in the objects (`.gnu.lto_*` section counts), not from flag strings: default `server.o` has 0 sections, `-O3` `server.o` has 281.
